The vacuous JaCoCo `compile`/`compile.gateway` rules are not placed here. This clarification does not change AC-12's meaning.

   **AC-12 observation obligation — R10-rev2-2: the unattributed run-3 pre-Ready `INTERNAL_ERROR` (inherited from STUDIO-OBS-012A; architect ruling `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-012A/root/architect-ruling-r10-rev2.json` R10-rev2-2, classification rule 3, 2026-10-13).** In one 012A full-class run (r8-selfstop C-run3), `GuidedPublicationReplayIT` C6 kind 1 (`actualPublishedNativePasswordStepDeadlinePreservesUnknownEffect`) ended pre-Ready as outer `RUN_ERROR` / `errorMessage` `INTERNAL_ERROR`, about 1.7 s after acceptance. The child left through its own `process.exit`. There was no `run.cancel_requested` and no host sleep. The cause is not attributed. The ruling's two candidates are (a) the child sent `INTERNAL_ERROR` for an unmapped pre-Ready exception, or (b) the parent rejected the child's result as a protocol violation. Details: `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-012A/r10-instrumentation/ledger.json` (`R10b_run3Recovery`) and `r10-rev2/ledger.json` (`openItem_R10rev2_2`, `fullClassRuns_C3`). It did not recur in the two credited 012A full-class runs with the diagnostic active, so it stays **open**: a CONCERNS item on the 012A gate (owner @qa), carried here by @po. The ruling records the run-3 outcome as fail-closed and conformant (no browser start, no strand, no USED audit). The open question is diagnosability, not security. This obligation does not change AC-12's meaning.

   a. **Keep the diagnostic active.** 012A added a permanent wrapper-only diagnostic to `GuidedPublicationReplayIT` (final 012A test sha256 `0f80cb54…`). Any OBS-009 edit to that class, including the AC-6 d tripwire change, keeps it working. Do not remove or weaken it, and do not replace it with the in-memory `childRuntime.js` load hook that the ruling forbids.

   b. **Where to look, for every OBS-009 full-class `GuidedPublicationReplayIT` run (all cases, normal order, no isolation), including the AC-12 backend suite on the final tree:**
      - failed-test-state evidence: `safeExecutionMetadata` attaches `ipcDiagnostics` per execution. It holds the IPC message kinds the parent received, the child's result `errorCode`, whether the result is Result3 schema-valid, the parent `handle.kill()` call-site label (`violation-catch`, `performStop-grace`, `other`), and the child exit code and signal.
      - the class temp directory: `ordinary-ipc-parent.jsonl` (parent side) and `ordinary-ipc-child.jsonl` (child side: `readySent`, `resultSent`, result `ok`, result `errorCode`), joined by pid and execution id.
      - per C6 case: the count of `INTERNAL_ERROR` pre-Ready ends, violation-catch kills and schema-invalid results. Record the run count as a denominator. The ruling does not fix a number of runs.

   c. **Classification if it recurs (ruling R10-rev2-2 `classificationRules`; stop and report in every case):**
      - The diagnostic shows a parent `violation-catch` kill, a result that is not schema-valid, or a missing result after a `launch` was processed: instrumentation or IPC interference. That is a **test defect**. Fix it in the test wrapper. Product files stay unchanged unless @architect rules otherwise.
      - The child sent `INTERNAL_ERROR` with no violation: an unmapped child pre-Ready exception. **Escalate to @architect** with the evidence. The architect decides whether it is product or preload, based on which preload wrappers were active.
      - A recurrence the diagnostic cannot classify stops crediting and goes to @architect (same rule as 012A C4).

      *(2026-10-18, @po (Pax): **named baseline record, not a recurrence.** Read literally, bullet 1 ("a missing result after a `launch` was processed") matches one record that is present in every `test`-lane full-class run: the childCut stop case's `performStop-grace` SIGKILL. Its identity is case 7, c6 2, ordinal 9, kill label `performStop-grace`, signal `SIGKILL`, `results` 0, no child line, and `received` = ready, step-started, step-finished, step-started (`resultSchemaValid` is `null`, not `false`). Lane S-W3 found the same record in 28 of 28 `test`-lane runs on this host (2026-10-16 01:58 through 2026-10-18), and the 012A credited runs show the same 29 parent / 28 child asymmetry (`docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-009/execution-20261018/s-w3/receipt.md:70`, `:80`, and finding 4 at `:114`). A record that matches every one of these fields is that baseline: it is not a recurrence, it is not classified under this bullet, and it is counted under `performStop-grace` kills. A record that differs in any field goes through c as written. It is not a `violation-catch` kill and not an `INTERNAL_ERROR`. That it exists by design of the cut case is S-W3's inference [I]. @qa may override this at G9.)*

   d. **Closure.** The item closes when OBS-009's full-class runs show no recurrence with the diagnostic active, or when an attributed occurrence is fixed. Until then it is reported as open in the OBS-009 evidence and QA Results. It is never reported as passed by omission.

   e. **Candidate only, not an obligation:** the ruling names a child-side, value-free exception-class log as a candidate for OBS-009, to go with R10-OBS-2 (AC-11 i). It is not in scope until @architect rules it in.

   **AC-12 obligation — P4/P5: standing test-infra risks for suite stability (inherited from STUDIO-OBS-012A; architect gate `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-012A/root/architect-gate.json` ruling 7 / placement P4, ruling 2 residual risk / placement P5, ruling 5 test-infra ownership, 2026-10-13).** These are environment and test-harness risks. They are not product defects. The product fix for the 012A port collision (`LocalEmbeddedPostgresConfig`, range 20000-32767, dual-stack exclusive probe) is complete. Each item below exists so that an AC-12 red run is attributed correctly, never credited by omission and never blamed on product without a check. This block does not change AC-12's meaning.

   f. **Host-port collision class (P4).** On macOS/BSD, a more specific `127.0.0.1:P` or `::1:P` listener shadows Docker Desktop's `*:P` for loopback clients. Any stale local JVM or postgres in the ephemeral range can therefore redirect a Testcontainers JDBC connection. 012A run 5 was aborted this way: a stale Gradle daemon still listened on `127.0.0.1:52100`. The risk lives mainly on developer and agent hosts, because CI agents are ephemeral. **Classification rule (gate ruling 7 / P4):** if a run fails with a connect `EOFException`, `role "test" does not exist`, or a connect hang after "Container is started", run `lsof -nP -iTCP -sTCP:LISTEN` and look for a `127.0.0.1`-only (or `::1`-only) java/postgres listener in the ephemeral range **before** attributing the failure to product. If such a listener holds the port, record the run as a host collision with the `lsof` line and re-run it after host cleanup. Such a run neither counts as a product failure nor earns credit. If no such listener exists, the failure is treated as a product or test failure and handled normally. Agents never kill host processes; cleanup needs user authorization (gate G4).

   g. **QA preflight (P4).** Before every AC-12 backend suite run and every @qa re-run, record the `lsof -nP -iTCP -sTCP:LISTEN` listing and any stale Gradle daemons. The root orchestration preflight (`.aiox/sdc/STUDIO-OBSERVED-ORCH/gradle-locked.sh`) warns on stale Gradle daemons and loopback-only listeners. That script is root tooling, not a story file. If it is absent, record the preflight by hand.

   h. **Latent in-repo hazard (P4).** `GuidedPlanningIntegrationTest` picks `RUNNER_PORT` via `new ServerSocket(0)` (~~`:2708-2709`~~ `:3652-3653`, readiness probe `:3856`, on 2026-10-16, PO delta D-8) and probes readiness on `127.0.0.1` only. Fix it so that a loopback-only collision cannot redirect or falsely satisfy the probe. The approach is @dev's and is named in evidence. Assertions stay unchanged.

   i. **Explicit runner-dist dependency (P5).** The backend guided ITs (`GuidedBridgeIntegrationTest`, `GuidedPlanningIntegrationTest`, `GuidedPublicationReplayIT`) spawn whatever untracked `studio-runner/dist` exists. Backend Gradle has no dependency on the runner build. Make the dependency explicit, either with a Gradle task that builds or verifies `dist` or with a recorded digest check before the guided ITs. Each guided IT run records the digest of the `dist` it spawned. QA credits a re-run only when that digest matches `dist` rebuilt from the final `src`. This also keeps the 012A R4 C2 proof for `guidedMain.ts` (gate ruling 2) from being lost silently if those ITs are moved or skipped.

   j. **Chromium startup hang as a test-infra event.** If a guided IT fails during browser startup before the AC-11 k bound exists, or with that bound in place, check for the `xpc_atfork_child` / `wait4` signature and for orphaned `checks-guided-*` processes before attributing the failure. Record such a run by name. It is not credited, and it is re-run after host cleanup.

   **AC-12 i addendum — `GuidedBridgePlannedIT` (from STUDIO-OBS-015 @architect bridge ruling `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-015/execution-20261015/backend/w2-window01/aria-bridge-ruling01/ruling.md` §4.6 and §5, 2026-10-16; placed by @po (Pax), record `execution-20261015/po-bridge-disclosure/`).** STUDIO-OBS-015 moves 16 methods of `GuidedBridgeIntegrationTest` into the new `GuidedBridgePlannedIT` under the same names, which leaves 9 in the old class. The new class spawns the same untracked `studio-runner/dist`. It therefore joins the guided ITs named in i:
   - Every run of it records the digest of the `dist` it spawned.
   - QA credits a re-run only under i's existing rule.
   - The AC-12 backend suite on the final tree names both bridge classes, with 9 and 16 methods. A run of `GuidedBridgeIntegrationTest` alone is not the full bridge corpus.

   This addendum does not change AC-12's meaning or i's rule.

   **AC-12 classification rule — the D4 15 s edge (from @architect (Aria) `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-015/execution-20261015/aria-d4-launch-deadline-ruling01/ruling-15s-edge.md` §4 item 2; root decision (yolo) 2026-10-17, `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBSERVED-NEXT/execute-015-008/CLAUDE-RESUME-STATE.md` "UPDATE ~14:35" item 3; placed by @po (Pax) 2026-10-17).** It applies to every AC-12 run and every @qa re-run that spawns the guided runner. The rule, verbatim: "In a gate, a bootstrap `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME` whose `messageCode` is `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME`, paired with a runner `GUIDED_START_FAILED` line whose `elapsedMs` is ≥ 15 000, is D4 case (a) or (b). It is not REL-001 recurring, and it is not a backend transport defect. Read the first such line. A retry produces a second `CONTEXT_LOST` diagnostic with a small `elapsedMs`."
   - Record such a run by name, with the first line and its `elapsedMs`. It is not counted as a REL-001 recurrence (AC-11 REL-001 pointer) and not as a backend failure.
   - Every other `GUIDED_START_FAILED` line goes through the AC-11 REL-001 pointer. The rule does not credit a run that failed for another reason, and f–j still apply.
   - This rule does not change AC-12's meaning.

## Dev Notes

### What is true today (verified 2026-10-09)

~~`studio-runner/Dockerfile:59`~~ `studio-runner/Dockerfile:61` *(2026-10-16, PO delta D-4)* ships `dist/main.js` only; `run/guidedMain.ts` is a ~~33-line~~ 37-line *(D-4)* bootstrap reading `STUDIO_GUIDED_CONFIG_FILE`; Helm has `studio-runner-{deployment,networkpolicy,scaledobject,service}.yaml` and no guided template. mTLS + RS256 exist in code (`GuidedInternalSecurity.java`, `guided/transport.ts`) but no profile or chart supplies key, issuer or truststore. Runner `server/server.ts` is plaintext `node:http`; `StudioRunnerProperties.validate()` accepts `http://`. `GuidedSessionReaper` and `GuidedTranslationCoordinator` are `@Scheduled` without a profile. `ConfiguredBackgroundAuthorization` grants only under `local`/`test`; secret-use authorization has only the dev-config source (GA C-2). No heap/core-dump settings in repo. Evaluation harness `studio-runner/evaluation/{cli,fixture-server,oracle,report,lifecycle}.mjs`, co-run `tools/corun.sh`/`corun-report.mjs` exist — extend, do not fork. `GuidedProviderEvaluationTest.java:58-84` skips when no provider env is set.

### Decisions pending (written to the stated default)

| Question | Default applied here | ACs that change if decided otherwise |
|---|---|---|
| **Q2 — Scheduled observed runs fill secrets with no live user (ARCH-9).** | Adopt parity with the existing scheduler via `ConfiguredBackgroundAuthorization` (design §8 Q2); G-3 holds regardless. | AC-6 becomes "observed schedules stay refused by design; emitters remain (OBS-011 keeps them); G-3 still recorded". |
| Corpus composition beyond the ADR's named cases (enterprise patterns). | Included as named cases fixed by @qa at T0 (panel QA gap). | AC-7 corpus list only; the ≥ 20 × 3 and 90 %/hard gates are the ADR's and do not change. |

Q4 (runner-terminated TLS + client certificate) is **decided** (ADR-SC-005 default; design §8).

### Thresholds and honesty rules

The gate is the ADR's (≥ 20 × 3, ≥ 90 %, zero false-pass, zero leak, latency/cost published with denominators). Do not promise latency or cost targets from unmeasured data; sample-based results do not prove arbitrary-site correctness; an absent staging application or gateway owner leaves AC-7(b)/AC-8 "unrun, owner X". The pilot cannot open on an unrun gate.

### Carried note — per-session model budget calibration (from STUDIO-OBS-012A DECISION-1 / F-1, 2026-10-13, @pm)

This is a backlog/calibration note. It is **not** an AC change, and AC-7's gate, corpus and thresholds are unchanged. The consensus plan [§8.2 amendment](../../specs/studio-observed-authoring/consensus-plan.md) raises the per-session input ceiling `max-model-input-tokens-total` from 128000 to 262144 as a byte-denominated reservation bound that has been checked only on fixtures. It is **not** a measured real-workload capacity. Use the AC-7 real-model runs, specifically the login prefix (OBS-007 fixture and staging) plus N steps, to recalibrate the per-session model budgets together:
- the input total: reserved bytes versus provider-reported tokens per request and per session;
- the output total: under production defaults, 32,000 / 4,096 per call limits a session to 7 proposals (`GuidedDefaultModelReservationIT`), so `max-model-proposals: 100` is unreachable (architect finding F-1, [architect-rulings-d1-d2.json](../../qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-012A/root/architect-rulings-d1-d2.json)).

Publish the measured per-run reserved and reported input/output with the AC-7 token denominators. Any change to a bound goes back to @architect as a ruling with @pm acknowledgement. Per-CSI aggregate budgets remain STUDIO-OBS-014 AC-8.

Values land through the ruling's §7 after G9. See "PO notes — MODEL-SIZING ruling acknowledged (2026-10-18)".)*

### Ruling — Chromium startup hang seen in 012A (owned here; architect gate 2026-10-13)

This replaces the 0.3.6 pointer, which said ownership was not ruled. The architect gate `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-012A/root/architect-gate.json` ruling 5 (placements P3, P4) ruled that the hang does not block 012A. **Product ownership: STUDIO-OBS-009** (AC-11 k). **Test-infra ownership: STUDIO-OBS-009 AC-12** (f–j). **Host cleanup: the user** (gate G4; agents do not kill processes).

What happened (`docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-012A/run4-residual/ledger.json` failure item 2, `latentNotFixed`): in 012A run 4, Chrome for Testing (Playwright chromium-1243) hung during startup. Its forked child was blocked in the fork child handler (`xpc_atfork_child` → `__psynch_mutexwait`), and the parent waited in `wait4`. No bootstrap callback was sent, and `GuidedPlanningIntegrationTest#genuineDelayedOldCallbackRequiresAcknowledgedFinalMetadata [1]` failed after 30.86 s. 012A did not touch the guided launch: `guidedMain.ts` is byte-identical to T0, and the 012A change to `browser.ts` was a removed type import.

The gate's facts:
- **The launch is bounded only by Playwright's default.** `launchGuidedBrowser` (~~`browser.ts:533`~~ `browser.ts:532` on 2026-10-16, PO delta D-6) has no Studio-owned timeout. The only bound is `DEFAULT_PLAYWRIGHT_LAUNCH_TIMEOUT` = 180 s (`playwright-core/lib/coreBundle.js:4595`), 12× the Java 15 s `pushContext` deadline. It is not literally unbounded.
- **Teardown leaves browser processes.** The guided worker launches Chromium in-process and has no process group. A test that force-kills the node worker before Playwright's 180 s timeout can reap the group leaves the Chromium tree alive. Two `checks-guided-*` processes from 15:35:54 were still alive on the host when the gate was written.

Inferred by the gate, not verified: the deadlock is in Chromium's fork child handler, not in repository code. The failure is fail-closed: the API side is bounded at 15 s, and nothing is learned or published. The fix direction is the replay path's discipline (AC-11 k), which belongs where the guided worker becomes the one runner process (AC-1).

### Repository conventions

Deployment via `helm-chart/put-checks/`; runner config keys are an allowlist (`server/config.ts` `KEY_SPECS`); backend profiles `application-{api,worker,prod,staging,dev,local,test}.yml`; all backend Gradle runs serialized; never mark an unavailable service, model call, staging application or production image as passed; record skips and unavailable services by name.

## Testing

Deployment tier: `studio-runner/test/deployment/*` (chart render, env keys, TLS/dump lint); backend startup/profile tests for `validate()`, G-3 refusal and `@Scheduled` profile gates; self-signed-CA IT for the TLS hop; Vault policy test against a dev Vault (skip honestly if unavailable, named); evaluation harness runs with the corpus manifest; co-run via `tools/corun.sh`; full backend/runner/frontend suites; Playwright E2E against the deployed stack. @qa independently reproduces: one guided run with zero finalize receipts, one incremental run, two replays, the revocation case, the mid-step cancel, the runner-kill case and the canary sweep.

**Added 2026-10-16 (@po, PO deltas D-14 and D-15).** "Full backend suites" means the five Gradle tasks named in the AC-12 clarification (`test`, `guidedIncrementalTest`, `crossReplicaTest`, `jacocoTestCoverageVerification`, `requireFreshGuidedPartitions`), credited only as full-class or full-lane runs. After STUDIO-OBS-008 U2 lands, the frontend suites run under pnpm: `pnpm run test` and `pnpm run test:e2e`, with exact `@playwright/test@1.63.0`. On 2026-10-16 that migration had not landed (`frontend/package-lock.json` present, no `frontend/pnpm-lock.yaml`). The runner stays on npm (`studio-runner/package-lock.json`, Dockerfile `npm ci`, `tools/corun.sh`).

## CodeRabbit Integration

### Story Type Analysis

Primary: Deployment/Infrastructure. Secondary: Security, Integration. Complexity: high.

### Specialized Agents

@dev owns execution; @devops co-executes AC-1…AC-5 (image, chart, TLS, policy, profiles); @architect provides the technical gate; independent @qa fixes the corpus, reproduces critical paths and owns the SDC verdict and Done transition.

### Quality Gates

- [ ] Applicable pre-commit checks and focused review recorded. *(2026-10-23, @po (Pax), PO closure: **deferred to STUDIO-OBS-016 AC-13 f, g (local focused checks recorded in QA rounds 073 and 075; full regression descoped by the user)**; not passed, not waived. See "PO closure — 2026-10-23".)*
- [ ] Independent SDC QA review with requirement-to-evidence mapping. *(2026-10-23, @po (Pax), PO closure: **deferred to STUDIO-OBS-016 AC-12 (local-scope QA PASS rounds 073 and 075)**; not passed, not waived. See "PO closure — 2026-10-23".)*
- [ ] Pre-deployment review by @devops before any shared-environment rollout; G-1/G-2/G-3 asserted. *(2026-10-23, @po (Pax), PO closure: **deferred to STUDIO-OBS-016 AC-14, AC-1, AC-22**; not passed, not waived. See "PO closure — 2026-10-23".)*

### Self-Healing and Focus Areas

QA full review: three iterations, 30 minutes. Focus: TLS on every secret-bearing hop, fail-closed defaults, one process/one trust chain, honest evidence with denominators, hard gates at zero. Record an unavailable CodeRabbit tool and use the configured independent review fallback.

## PO note — accessibility scope is functional only (ADR-SC-006, 2026-10-17)

Recorded by @po (Pax), append-only. Source: the user decision of 2026-10-17, relayed by the lead. Verbatim: "Remove the a11y (accessibility) that are not must have, for instance we don't have to have shortcuts and we dont have handicap users for now" and "Persist this a11y decisions for the core project documents so we avoid then, and also remove for future stories (e.g: 08,09,10 and 11)". Project rule: `docs/architecture/ADR-SC-006-accessibility-scope-functional-only.md` (Accepted). AC text, tasks and Status (**Ready**) are unchanged.

- **Result.** This story has no accessibility AC, and nothing in it is struck. @po ran `grep -niE 'wcag|accessib|a11y|axe|screen reader|aria-|reduced-motion|keyboard'` on 2026-10-17 before this note and got 18 hits. Each was checked:
  - **14 are ruling file names, not a11y.** These are `aria-*-ruling01` paths, from Aria (@architect), at `:40`, `:131`, `:264`, `:304`, `:351`, `:376`, `:381`, `:382`, `:420`, `:427`, `:628`, `:629`, `:681` and `:683`. Decision: KEEP.
  - **1 is a substring match.** `:49` "relaxed" contains "axe". Decision: KEEP.
  - **3 are runner target-page attributes,** product behaviour: `:144`, `:154` and `:174` in FOCUS-MUTATION-01 (`aria-expanded`, `aria-activedescendant`, `aria-describedby`, `aria-disabled` in the `POST_FOCUS_CHANGED` guard and its inert-attribute allowlist). ADR-SC-006 "Not affected" names this allowlist. Decision: KEEP.
- **AC-12 real-browser E2E: KEEP.** It extends OBS-008's Playwright config with a functional journey (author → Run → … → video seek). It does not extend an a11y spec or audit, so nothing is struck. OBS-008 creates no a11y spec (OBS-008 "PO scope decision 2", X8).
- **Frontend regression suites (AC-12, T5): KEEP.** The suite includes the existing jsdom `studioWcagAudit.test.tsx`. It runs as a regression guard only (ADR-SC-006 "Still required"). Its result is a regression check, never an a11y conformance claim.
- **Going forward.** No a11y work from the ADR's "Do NOT" list (live regions, shortcuts or custom keyboard navigation, ARIA-only annotations, reduced motion, contrast or target-size checks, new audit surfaces or real-browser a11y specs, a11y reviews or gate tools) may be added to this story without a new user decision that supersedes ADR-SC-006. Ordinary keyboard input that is part of a test step's meaning (for example, a runner "press Enter" action) is product behaviour, not a11y.

## Carried items (inbound from 008): append-only

| C-OBS008-4 | 2026-10-17 | @architect (Aria) ruling `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-008/execution-20261017/rulings/w5-unreadable-and-alias.md` §4, "Carry to root" (sha256 `3fbf9dcb…`). Dex W5-2 in `w5-reviews/dex-technical-review.md` (`06b2c52d…`), with the probe `w5-reviews/logs/probe-backend-ambiguity.log`. Uma `w5-reviews/U1-spec.md` §7.4 (`939719c1…`). Quinn `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-008/qa-gate-20261017/gate-draft.md` §5 item 4 and REQ-002 (`e89fbd46…`) | Lane C9 (@dev contract integrator) with @architect | `startGuidedSession` can answer `422 INVALID_CONTRACT` / `AUTHORING_REFERENCE_AMBIGUOUS`, but `docs/specs/studio/openapi.yaml` declares no 422 for it. | Lane C9; T1; the contract-pin rule that AC-1 P2 already uses | Open |

**C-OBS008-4 details.**

a. **Ruling, quoted** (Aria, §4).
   - "The real refusal is at start with HTTP 422, which `startGuidedSession` does not declare. It lists 400/401/403/409/429 (`openapi.yaml:1345-1416`), and `grep "'422'"` finds 0. A mirror check would invent a status (Article IV)."
   - "**Carry to root (backend or contract owner, not U1):** declare the start 422, or remap it. Then add to the *start* mirror (`guidedHandlers.ts:562`, not `handlers.ts`) exactly the four refusals listed above. The same carry covers the Variables dialog, which can still save an ambiguous set by hand."

b. **Facts, verified by @po on 2026-10-17.** Re-verify them at HEAD before C9 starts.
   - **The four refusals.** `backend/src/main/java/com/citi/put/checks/studio/authoring/IncrementalAuthoringIdentity.java` (sha256 `80b943dc…`) refuses in four cases:
     - a `secretRef` without a pattern-valid `secretId` (`:63-70`);
     - a duplicate `name` (`:71-73`);
     - two `secretRef`s with the same `secretId` but different declarations (`:77-80`);
     - a `secretRef` whose `secretId` equals the name of another declaration that is not a `secretRef` with that same `secretId` (`:83-90`).

     All four throw `ambiguous()`, which is `new GuidedSessionException("INVALID_CONTRACT", 422, "AUTHORING_REFERENCE_AMBIGUOUS", false, null)` (`:379-382`).
   - **Reached at start.** `GuidedSessionService.java:643` (`access.resolve`) calls `guided/translation/GuidedProductionSessionAccess.java:85` (`classifier.classify`), which calls `GuidedSourceClassifier.java:69-78` (`IncrementalAuthoringIdentity.resolve`). The controller answers `failure.status()` (`GuidedSessionController.java:103-105`). Dex ran the real `resolve` and it refused his rules 4a and 4b [V]. The HTTP path is [I] in Dex's review and [V] by reading in Aria's.
   - **Contract.** `docs/specs/studio/openapi.yaml` sha256 `b5ecdcad…`. `startGuidedSession` is at `:1298`, and its responses run from `'400'` at `:1345` to `'429'` at `:1409`. `grep -c "'422'"` returns 0. The file's only other `422` (`:281`) belongs to the internal `admitObservedExecution`.
   - **Pins.** `StudioContractTest.java:90-91` `BASE_SHA256` and `studio-runner/test/run/confinement.test.ts:294` both equal `b5ecdcad…`.
   - **Start mirror.** `frontend/src/services/mocks/studio/guidedHandlers.ts` (sha256 `a32125ba…`) starts its handler at `:562` (`http.post(BASE, … mutate('start', …))`). It has no ambiguity check: a grep for `AMBIGUOUS` in it and in `guidedFixtures.ts` returns 0.
   - **Today's UI.** A start 422 falls to the undeclared-refusal view, with the code shown and no success claim (Quinn gate-draft §2.2, "Fail-closed paths", [V]). The STUDIO-OBS-008 picker no longer creates an ambiguous set, because U1 W5-2 made `declareInsertedSecret` refuse it. The Variables dialog can still save one by hand (Aria §4; Quinn REQ-002, low).

c. **Fix.** @architect chooses between two options, and @po does not pick.
   - **(1) Declare the 422** on `startGuidedSession` in `openapi.yaml`. This is a contract change:
     - re-anchor both pins, `StudioContractTest` `BASE_SHA256` and `confinement.test.ts` `BASE_SHA256`, with the old and new digests quoted;
     - `node tools/check-studio-observed-contracts.cjs` and the generator `--check` pass;
     - 0 operations are added (C9 exit condition).
   - **(2) Change the backend status** of these refusals to one the operation already declares. [I] The document and the pins may then stay unchanged; @architect confirms. The edit is in `IncrementalAuthoringIdentity.java`, outside C9's write list, so @architect names its owner.
   - **Then, for either option:** add exactly the four backend refusals to the start mirror in `guidedHandlers.ts`, with the status and code the contract then declares. Add a test per case, with each mirror body validated against the v3 schemas as `guidedHandlers.test.ts` already does. Do not change `handlers.ts` for this (Aria §4).

d. **Variables dialog.** This carry covers it too (Aria §4). The ruling names no fix for the dialog, and @po does not pick one. Until one is ruled, the start refusal shows the code and nothing is lost (Quinn REQ-002).

e. **One re-anchor.** [AUTO-DECISION] If option (1) is chosen, does the 422 go into the same C9 unit as the AC-1 P2 `RuntimeVersions` edit? → Recommended yes (reason: the digest and both pins then move once, which matches the C9 exit condition in `lanes.json`, "both pins re-anchored in one unit"). Today C9's write list says `RuntimeVersions` only, so the coordinator widens it. The coordinator may override this, and @po does not edit `lanes.json`.

f. **Single owner and sequencing.** `openapi.yaml`, both pins and `guidedHandlers.ts` are STUDIO-OBS-008 files. C9 starts after 008 Done ("PO T0 outcomes", Order; `implementation-plan.md`: "`openapi.yaml`, `StudioContractTest` pin, `confinement.test.ts` pin: 008 L0 → C9").

g. **Not decided here and never dropped.** No source sets a release gate for this item; Quinn rates REQ-002 low. If it is not done in this story, T6 reports it open with owner C9 and @architect, and it is never dropped silently.

**a. Known limitation KL-009-REVOKE-UNLEARNED (disclosure; Aria pre-F-merge ruling 2, §2.3).** A revocation the runner could not learn through either channel is reported as an error with no named code.
- The case: a revocation is recorded, the runner's control read is lost, and no later acknowledgement arrives. The run ends outer `RUN_ERROR` with `errorMessage` `CANCELLED`, and the API shows `ERROR` with a null `error_code`, not `AUTHORIZATION_REVOKED`. The ledger still carries `revoked_at`, which is write-once.
- It fails closed: the run stopped and the secret was not used further.
- It is not fixed. Ruling 2 rejects mapping `RUN_ERROR` + `CANCELLED` + revoked to `AUTHORIZATION_REVOKED`: it contradicts T0 ruling A12 and lane B's pin, it would need a cross-clock ordering against `SecretRevocationSweep`, and it would re-open a closed lane. The fixtures-tier test `observed-secret-control > lost-control-body` is updated to that shape at F-merge (ruling §2.2). `slow-revoked` and `progress-revoked` keep `CANCELLED`, unedited.
- Not checked: whether the secret-use audit row names the revocation on this path ([I] in the ruling).
- A time-ordered cause mapping is a GA backlog candidate only if pilot evidence shows the case. @po has not opened a backlog row for it.
- [AUTO-DECISION] Where does the line go? → Here, as a dated known-limitation note, and the AC-11 text is not edited (reason: the ruling asks for "one known-limitation line", and the behaviour already matches A12, so AC meaning does not change). STUDIO-OBS-010's release-record row for it is 010's to add; this pass does not edit 010.

**b. New external prerequisite U23: the guided callback and controller token issuer (Aria W1.5 ruling Q6).**
- **What.** One external issuer that writes three audience-scoped token files: the runner's `STUDIO_RUNNER_GUIDED_CALLBACK_TOKEN_FILE`, and the backend's two controller token files (`application.yml:200` `controller-token-file` and the second file the ruling cites at `:202`). Tokens are RS256, and each lives at most 300 s (`GuidedInternalSecurity.java:205-210`, `expires - issued <= 300`). One issuer key verifies both roles, `owning-worker` and `backend-controller` (`:196-201`). The issuer refreshes Secrets mounted as directories, with no `subPath`.
- **No in-pod minting.** Ruling Q6 co-signs Gage's refusal of every in-pod design: a signing key in the pod that renders untrusted pages could also mint `backend-controller` tokens; a sidecar breaks the one-container bound; a static Secret expires within 300 s.
- **Owner.** External (Platform/SRE identity, per the ruling), **not named**. It is not ED-02 until someone confirms ED-02.
- **Needed before** G-1 and any guided-enabled shared environment. It is **not** needed for the local 009 ACs. D2 makes the guided render fail on an empty token Secret name.
- **Register.** Ruling Q6 suggested id U21, which the 2026-10-17 batch had already used (ED-02 policy note). The next free id is **U23**. It is appended to `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-009/prep-20261016/prerequisite-register.json` under the new key `w2Batch20261018`, with `satisfied: false`. Observation [V, read by @po]: `helm-chart/put-checks/values-uat.yaml:184` and `:186` already name `checks-studio-guided-callback-token-uat` and `checks-studio-guided-controller-tokens-uat`; those Secrets are U23's output.

**c. U14 and the TF-5 grant scope (lane DE finding F1).** A non-owner runtime role needs much more than "SELECT on the view". The DE receipt F1: "A non-owner runtime role needs block 1 in full: 17 new relations, plus UPDATE on `studio_guided_materializations` and `studio_guided_model_attempts`, DELETE on the three publication tables (the V19 pruner) and SELECT on `studio_guided_translation_plans`/`_state`." F2: the deferred trigger `studio_015_version_commit` reads the view too, so materialization writes also need the view grant. F4: a split role model also needs Flyway, and partition maintenance, to run as the owner.
- The grant to apply, if U14 confirms a non-owner role, is [`runtime-role-grants-v19-v22.sql`](../../qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-009/execution-20261018/de/runtime-role-grants-v19-v22.sql) (`02c13ee6…`), applied by the DBA through the environment's process. Lane DE shipped no V23, and head stays V22.
- The TF-5 wording "grant SELECT on `studio_guided_materialization_snapshots`" (AC-4 TF-5; T2) is not reworded. When U14 is answered, read it as "apply the prepared grant", which the DE IT proves exact for V19 through V22. [AUTO-DECISION] Is this an AC meaning change? → No (reason: TF-5 b already says "check the V19-through-head privilege delta", and the prepared grant is that delta). The coordinator may override this.

**d. UAT readiness before release (lane D2 finding F4; for STUDIO-OBS-010 readiness).** `values-uat.yaml` already enables the runner. UAT stays in `ContainerCreating` until two per-environment Secrets exist, `checks-studio-runner-tls-uat` and `checks-studio-backend-runner-tls-uat`, and the controller certificate pin (`studioRunner.tls.controllerCertificateFingerprint256`) is supplied. All three are external to this story. @po does not edit STUDIO-OBS-010; its release readiness reads this note. Runbook `docs/runbooks/studio-runner.md` §14.4 records the same break (prep-devops F3).

**a. RUN-ORPHAN-01: the replay child outlives a runner SIGKILL (carried residue; Aria pre-G9 ruling §1, lane F finding F-A).**
- **Fact** (`f/receipt.md:130`, [V] by lane F): after a runner SIGKILL, the replay child and its browser tree stay alive until the in-flight step ends: 30.1 s at a 30 s step timeout, and 10.0 s twice at 10 s. A guided browser dies with the runner in about 0.3 s. Nothing in `child.ts`, `childRuntime.ts` or `launcher.ts` handles parent death (ruling §1.1). On a host, the orphan can live up to the clamped step timeout, 120 s or more ([V] ruling §1.1).
- **Ruling** (`w3/pre-g9-rulings.md:36-52`): "Not required in 009. Accept and carry it as `RUN-ORPHAN-01`." AC-11's runner-kill leg (zero repeated dispatches, `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME`, no incorrectly published plan) is met. AC-11 has no "no surviving process" clause for the replay path, and AC-1 is about topology (ruling §1.2).
- **G9 wording** (condition 1): G9 credits the AC-11 runner-kill leg as "zero repeats, settled STALE". It does not credit "no survivor". F's §9 stays as written (`f/receipt.md:115`).
- **Owners.** The fix is @dev (runner). The in-pod check is @devops, and it rides with AC-1 PID-1 d on the deployed stack (condition 2, `:46-51`). The check: in a guided-enabled runner pod, start a replay run whose step never finishes, `kill -9` the runner pid from a debug container that shares the PID namespace, and record that no `node`, Chromium or `chrome-headless` process from that run is left in the pod's namespace or cgroup within 5 s, and that the container restarted.
- **Escalation.** If the in-pod check is not run, it is reported "unrun, owner @devops", never as a pass. If it shows survivors, RUN-ORPHAN-01 becomes a **GA blocker (GA plan §4.G)**, not a 009 blocker.
- **Closes when** both of these hold: (1) the runner change lands per ruling §1.4 (`:54-67`): `process.once('disconnect', …)` in `child.ts` `startChild` leads to `stop('CANCELLED')` and then a hard group-kill exit bound of ≤ 2 s; a 1 s `ppid` poll as fallback; a handled `error` event on `process`. Its tests are the `childRuntime` fake-channel unit test, a fixtures-tier real-Chromium parent SIGKILL with no survivor from the child's group within 3 s, and the F co-run `runnerKill` leg gaining a "no survivors within 3 s" invariant. (2) The @devops in-pod check records no survivors.
- **[AUTO-DECISION] 009 carried residue or an STUDIO-OBS-011 row?** → **009 carried residue**, recorded here with the owner and close condition above. It is not a 009 Done condition (reason: the ruling's condition 3 says @po places it as "a 009 residue that points to the next runner-hardening work, or to the GA plan §4.G item if no runner story is open". No runner-hardening story exists. STUDIO-OBS-011 is a behaviour-neutral sweep whose AC-7 exceptions are narrow and gate-ordered, and a parent-death exit on the kill path is a runner behaviour change that touches the AC-11 j / R10-OBS-1 path). The pointer for when it is picked up is GA plan §4.G. If no runner story is open by the OBS-010 release review, the OBS-010 AC-4 record lists it there as open, owner @dev (runner) and @devops. @po does not edit STUDIO-OBS-010 in this pass. The coordinator may override this.

**b. SEC-AUDIT-PREREADY-01: a pre-Ready recorded revocation audits `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME`, not `NOT_USED` (accepted; carried residue; Aria pre-G9 ruling §2, lane B-W3 finding 3).**
- **Fact** (`b/w3/receipt.md`, finding 3; ruling §2.1, [V]): the real runner's pre-Ready FINAL for ADJ-1 is `AUTHORIZATION_REVOKED`, accepted on the first attempt, and the terminal audit has one USAGE row, `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME`. The `RunFailure` path in `runCoordinator.ts` builds its outcome without `secretUse`, and `SecretAuditLifecycle.replayTerminal` maps a missing entry to `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME`. Secrets reach the child only in `Execute3`, after Ready, so `NOT_USED` would be factually true.
- **Ruling** (`w3/pre-g9-rulings.md:97-111`): "Accept and document it for 009. No change before G9." `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME` is "less precise, but not wrong": it never under-reports use, and no 009 AC asks for `NOT_USED` (§2.2). There is no security defect.
- **Disclosure.** For a revocation recorded before Ready, the secret-use audit reads `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME` for a secret that provably was never in the child. A reviewer must not read it as "maybe typed".
- **G9.** @qa credits the ADJ-1 leg on the observed `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME`, with `AUTHORIZATION_REVOKED` labelled. The joined IT's either-value assertion (B-W3 deviation 4) is accepted for 009 only and named as weak. The W2 A1 `NOT_USED` case proves only the backend mapping of a runner-supplied value. @qa's ADJ-1 security co-sign is still owed (`b/w3/receipt.md`, header).
- **Owner** @dev (runner), option A (the runner attests).
- **Closes when** the runner adds `secretUse` = `NOT_USED` for every declared secret on every `RunFailure` raised before `Execute3` was sent, keeps today's behaviour after `Execute3`, and the ADJ-1 joined IT (`PreReadyRevocationJoinedIT`) pins `NOT_USED` and stops accepting either value.
- **[AUTO-DECISION] Where?** → Here, as a 009 carried residue with a disclosure. It is not a 009 Done condition, and there is no STUDIO-OBS-011 row (reason: it is a runner behaviour change, so the same reasoning as a applies, and the ruling names no other home). It travels with RUN-ORPHAN-01 to the next runner-hardening work. Until then, the OBS-010 AC-4 record carries the disclosure line above. The coordinator may override this.

**c. AC-12 R10-rev2-2: the baseline `performStop-grace` SIGKILL at `GuidedPublicationReplayIT` case 7 is named** (lane S-W3 finding 4, `s-w3/receipt.md:80`, `:114`). The identity and the rule are in the dated note under AC-12 c. S-W3's run shows no recurrence (denominator: 1 full-lane run, 73 ordinary-child records). R10-rev2-2 stays **open** for @qa at G9, under d as written, and is not closed by this note.

**d. AC-11 R1 e (KL-015-R1 disclosure): closed on lane evidence.** The basis, the receipts and the revival condition are in the dated note under AC-11 R1 e. R1 a–c: `b/receipt.md:115`, run `obs009-b-04`. R1 d: `b/w3/receipt.md:63`, run `obs009-b-w3-04` on D_F `4add1307…`. Ruling: `w1.5/ruling-part2.md:88`. Credit for R1 a–d stays @qa's at G9.

**e. MODEL-SIZING is a T4 precondition (lane EP B7; owner @architect + @pm).**
- **Measurement** (`ep/receipt.md` §4, `:66-81`; `ep/b7-proposals.json`; local fixture provider only, so it can refute but never confirm). B7 **refutes** 7 proposals per session. A 10-step base learn (the edit-4 case) needs **13** proposals. At the 4096 production per-call ceiling that reserves 53,248 output tokens, which is **166 %** of the 32,000 session total. The 4096 column is arithmetic, not a run: the run used the fixture's 2048 ceiling, and the proposal count does not depend on the ceiling. Across 70 sessions the peak is 14, and 54 of 70 need more than 7.
- **The question for the ruling** (EP does not choose): either raise the per-session output total to at least 13 × 4096 = 53,248, plus room for the retries and clarifications that a real model adds, or lower the per-call ceiling to ≤ floor(32,000 / 13) = 2,461. The incremental lane already runs at 2048, which gives 15.
- **Placement.** It joins ED-01 (gateway owner, U2; price list U18) and budget headroom R (U17) as the three T4 preconditions: dated note on T4. Any change to a bound follows this story's "Carried note — per-session model budget calibration" (an @architect ruling with @pm acknowledgement). It also answers the open MODEL-SIZING follow-up in "Carried review follow-ups — 2026-10-14" with a measurement. That follow-up is not closed by this note; it closes with the ruling. Per-CSI aggregate budgets stay STUDIO-OBS-014 AC-8.
- **Not decided by @po:** the sizing value, and whether B7's fixture profile is representative.

**f. G9 C4 (runner deployment tier): the image-dependent tests and the D2 image smoke are owed on U22.** *(2026-10-18 update, @po (Pax): authorized; run pending after G9 C8. See the last bullet of this item.)*
- **Result, [V] read by @po** from `execution/obs009-g9-runner-deploy01-vitest.json`: 581 tests, **575 passed, 6 failed**, 0 skipped. `local-gate-20261018/status.txt` records `C4-runner-deployment exit=1`. Every one of the 6 failures is the deployment tier's image prerequisite: "the image `put-checks-studio-runner:10.18a-local` does not exist locally. Build it first (`ci/stages.md` stage 7, `docker build -t <tag> studio-runner/`) and pass the tag as `STUDIO_RUNNER_IMAGE`." The six are:
  - `test/deployment/chart.test.ts`: "story 10.18a — the rendering harness itself has the built image, or FAILS — AC-4a part 2 reads its facts out of it"; "AC-4a parts 1-2 — SEC-2 … no writable mount sits on the image's browser path or on its HOME"; "… the disjointness check is capable of failing"; "STUDIO-OBS-009 Q4 … declares none of them in the image under test either"; "… keeps the new writable mounts off the browser registry and HOME, on guided sets too";
  - `test/deployment/sec2.test.ts`: "SEC-2 — the shim browser registry attack, run in both directions has put-checks-studio-runner:10.18a-local, or FAILS".
- **Cause, relayed by the lead** and not checked by @po (no Docker command was run): no image built from the current Dockerfile exists. Building one needs U22, the user's Docker authorization (root decision R5, not decided; register `prep-20261016/prerequisite-register.json` U22, `NOT_SATISFIED`).
- **Owed on U22**, each "unrun, owner user (U22)" until authorized, never a pass:
  1. the six image-dependent deployment-tier tests above, re-run against an image built from the current `studio-runner/Dockerfile` (after the A6 CMD `sh -c "ulimit -c 0 && exec node dist/main.js"`);
  2. the D2 image smoke as U22 words it: `docker build`; `docker run --init` with guided mode on (pid ≠ 1 recorded); `docker run` without `--init` (`CONFIGURATION_DENIED` recorded); `docker stop` (`runner.draining` and `runner.drained` under the new CMD). This is AC-1 PID-1 c and the A6 drain re-record.
- **Not decided by @po:** whether C4 counts as met at G9 with these six named, or whether they go on the QA approved-skips list. That is @qa's call under Quinn's C-criteria. If U22 is refused, the six stay "unrun, no authorization" by name.
- **2026-10-18 update (@po (Pax), append-only): U22 is authorized; the run is pending after G9 C8.** The user chose "Authorize local build" (`execution-20261018/w0/decisions.md`, "U22 / R5 image half: user decision (2026-10-18)"). Items 1 and 2 above change from "owed on U22" to **"authorized; run pending after G9 C8"**. Until root records the build and the runs, each stays unrun by name, never a pass. ## PO notes — deployed-stack blocker and U22 authorization (2026-10-18)

Recorded by Pax (@po), append-only, on the lead's instruction of 2026-10-18, while G9 C8 was still running (no source, test, chart or spec file edited). No AC text is reworded, AC meaning is unchanged, Status stays **Ready**, and nothing is marked Done, passed or waived. Receipt: `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-009/execution-20261018/po-carry/receipt-3.md`.

**Sources (sha256 taken by @po 2026-10-18).** `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-010/live-surface-pre-g9-20261018/rulings/ruling.md` (Aria, §0 row B and §B1–§B5) `71832a0a42f2663673a77f7915d0ea917328f162d337902f17b6d2713f6635ce`; `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-009/execution-20261018/w0/decisions.md` (root, "U22 / R5 image half: user decision") `0f11e989ec04b43a881fe11dab414826a0c58338dac6910f3f2602e77ee12f48`.

**a. DEPLOY-OBSERVED-01: a chart-deployed stack cannot run observed work (carried residue against AC-1 / U4; Aria ruling B). The ID is @po's label.**
- **Facts, as ruled** ([V] by @architect, by code reading, not run; @po did not re-read the code anchors):
  - **Runner, 503.** `STUDIO_RUNNER_OBSERVED_VALIDATION_ENABLED` and `…_REPLAY_ENABLED` default to false (`studio-runner/src/server/config.ts:177-178`). The chart cannot set them: the closed list `acceptedEnvKeys` (`helm-chart/put-checks/templates/_helpers.tpl:162`) has no `OBSERVED_*` key, any other `runnerEnv` key fails the render (`:191-192`), and the runner Deployment has no `envFrom`. So on a chart-deployed runner, `POST /v2/validate-observed` answers 503 `OBSERVED_VALIDATION_DISABLED` (`routes.ts:213`) and `POST /v2/runs` answers 503 `OBSERVED_REPLAY_DISABLED` (`runCoordinator.ts:1048`, relayed at `routes.ts:219`).
  - **Backend, 409.** `studio.observed-replay.catalog-json` and `policies-json` default to `[]` (`application.yml:269-270`), and the chart renders neither. `ObservedReplayPins` then answers 409 `RUNTIME_INCOMPATIBLE` (`:33-35`, `:39-47`, `:67-79`) to publication, replay authorization, the guided production session, governed policy and capabilities.
  - **Consequence [I, @architect].** With the shipped chart a deployed stack cannot publish a learned plan, authorize a replay or run any Studio test.
- **What it blocks** (ruling §B3): AC-7 (the D-C benchmark "on the deployed stack"), the AC-12 deployed E2E, the G-1 deployed proof, and so **009 Done and the 010 pilot**. AC-1's one-Deployment sentence is met as routing only, not as a usable deployment. It does **not** block G9, the dated 010 Live surface list or the STUDIO-OBS-011 start.
- **Placement** (ruling §B4): a 009 residue, required before any deployed-stack leg (U4 bring-up, T4, AC-12 deployed). Not STUDIO-OBS-011 (a behaviour-neutral sweep) and not GA (the D-C gate runs before the pilot).
- **Owners.**
  1. **@devops (Gage): the chart change**, exactly as ruling §B5 items 1–9 and 11 give it: a top-level `studioObserved` block, off by default; the five `STUDIO_RUNNER_OBSERVED_*` keys made chart-managed; both flags rendered both ways; the file and digest keys only while a flag is on; the runner image pinned by digest while on; a read-only trust ConfigMap; the backend `catalog-json`/`policies-json` through `springProperties` from the same source; render guards G-O1…G-O4; `values-uat.yaml` turns both flags on with catalog and policies supplied at deploy time. Verification named in §B5 (not run): `chart.test.ts` cases (i)–(iii) and `backend-env.test.ts`; first deployed smoke: `/v2/validate-observed` does not answer 503. Timing: after G9 exits, before U4 is brought up.
  2. **@dev: the catalog-entry producer** (§B5 item 10), a runner script such as `studio-runner/scripts/print-observed-catalog.mjs` that prints the image's own `{profile, build}` entry from the `ObservedCoRunIT.java:290-310` derivation and validates it against the `BuildIdentity` schema. @devops then adds the CI step that runs it inside the pushed image.
  3. **@pm (Morgan): name the owner of the policy content** (`studioObserved.policies`, which applications and origins are trusted per environment). Aria's default, not adopted by @po: the pilot application owner with Security & Compliance, applied by @devops. **Open.** *(2026-10-18 update, @po (Pax): @pm answered in `docs/qa/evidence/STUDIO-OBS-010/live-surface-pre-g9-20261018/rulings/pm-ack.md` §3 (sha256 `cd618df7…`): the owner is **for the user to name**; @pm names no person or team. Recommended split (@pm, concurring with Aria; not adopted until the user names an owner): the **pilot application owner** writes and maintains the content (one named owner for UAT first, then one per later environment); **Security & Compliance** approves each policy file and each change; **@devops** applies the approved file at deploy time, never as runtime input; @pm records the named owner. It is an **AC-4 prerequisite for the STUDIO-OBS-010 pilot** (010 "PO notes — @pm acknowledgement of the pre-G9 rulings (2026-10-18)" item c). Until named, a UAT render with observed on fails G-O3, so U4 bring-up, T4 on the deployed stack, AC-7/AC-12 deployed legs and the pilot stay blocked; G9, the dated 010 list and STUDIO-OBS-011 are not. Still **open, owner: the user**. [AUTO-DECISION] Add it to `prep-20261016/prerequisite-register.json` next to U13/U14 now, as @pm suggests? → No (reason: the register is updated by root with evidence; the lead did not list it). It is flagged for root.)*
- **Closes when** the §B5 chart change and the catalog producer have landed with their named tests, @pm has named the policy-content owner, and a deployed smoke records that neither `/v2/validate-observed` nor `/v2/runs` answers 503 and that publication does not answer 409 for the catalog. Until then every deployed-stack leg reads "blocked by DEPLOY-OBSERVED-01, owner @devops", never a pass.
- **Knock-on (recorded, not decided by @po).** The chart edit moves the helm digest; the 010 Live surface chart categories are re-measured at the 009-Done re-date (ruling §B4, §A6). After the change, RKS-01..05 become live in a values file that enables them; they stay listed (§A6). UAT also needs the controller pin (OI-19, ruling §B6; 009 PO note d), so @devops documents both deploy-time inputs in one UAT note.

**b. U22: the user authorized the local runner image build, the C4 re-run and the D2 image smoke (2026-10-18).**
- **Decision, quoted** (`w0/decisions.md`): the user chose **"Authorize local build"**: a local `docker build` of `studio-runner/` with a fresh tag, no image pulls (BuildKit may fetch the Dockerfile syntax frontend, as the 015 builds did); then a C4 re-run at the G9 frozen digest with `STUDIO_RUNNER_IMAGE=<tag>`; then the D2 image smoke from `d2/pid1-launch-paths.md` §"The image smoke that is owed" (`--init` and without it, then `docker stop` / drain). All of it runs **after G9 C8 has ended**, with nothing else heavy alongside, and root records the build and the runs.
- **Effect on the earlier notes.** "PO notes — W3 carries and pre-G9 rulings" item f and the AC-1 PID-1 d pointer now read **"authorized; run pending after G9 C8"** instead of "owed on U22" (dated notes added there). The six image-dependent deployment-tier tests and the D2 image smoke stay unrun by name until root records them; nothing is credited by this note.
- **Still @qa's call:** whether the C4 re-run at the frozen digest replaces the G9 C4 exit 1, and how it is credited.
- **Register.** `prep-20261016/prerequisite-register.json` U22 still reads `NOT_SATISFIED`; @po did not edit it. [AUTO-DECISION] Leave the register to root, who records the build (reason: the lead did not list it, and the satisfied state is better recorded together with the run evidence).

### Local real-stack result

case437 (run `23208c4c-953b-4804-889c-86cbc0845c1a`, A `46d4a987034359d84a0eaf941f368ac6e59a7f28ad5f870b52f66608d096d79b`) is the **first complete local pass**: workflowCode COMPLETE, latestStage ordinary-replay, mutations start 2 / clarify 1 / finalize 2 / execute 1. Flow: full learn (9 attempts, 1 SUPERSEDED 45 s stall, 6 actions OK), the genuine step-5 clarification, publication 1, the middle edit to "Click Open in Primary workspace", the incremental relearn from step 5 with no question (5 attempts, 2 SUPERSEDED, 6 actions OK incl. the qualified step 5), publication 2 (epoch 2, rev 3 confirmed) and the ordinary replay PASSED (`lastRunStatus` PASSED). Screenshots 17 planned / 17 attempted / 17 released. Native recordings 07/11/13 were captured; the step+action DOM seeks were validated for the first learn, the incremental run and the ordinary replay; no UI error diagnostic. Sources: `G/round-066/projection437/projection-stdout.json`, collectors `G/round-065/devops-case437/diagnostic437/` (`result-a/b/m/t.json`), pair receipt 648fded4 (37/37), finalize 41/41. The UX expert's masked-PNG review (`G/round-066/ux-png-review/`, all 17 hashes matched) has **no blocking finding**: PASS 01, 02, 03, 05, 06, 11 step, 11 action, 14, 15, 16; PARTIAL 04, 07 step, 07 action, 08, 09, 10, 12.

The projection itself records masking INCONCLUSIVE, nativeVisualAssessment and nativeSeekVisualCorrectness UNASSESSED, humanC17 UNRUN, requesterReview PENDING and fullAcceptance false. This is one local synthetic-target run on a real Vertex stack with the harness call-seconds at 45; it is not a company D-C pass and not an acceptance.

### Product fixes this wave (each: evidence and QA gate in the RS71 task lines)

- (a) Runner `observationCollector` password-label fix, round 049 (QA PASS). Fixed the case431 loop.
- (b) Ruling a+b, rounds 053-057: V23 `rejection_reason` and the transient retry (QA PASS, 277 tests); applied at B0 (round 058).
- (c) Qualified-label product rule, round 057 (user decision "Product rule", 2026-10-23; QA PASS, recommendations R1-R6; delta G1/G2 accepted).
- (d) Runner recording-finalize fix, round 065: the hard stage no longer SIGKILLs ffmpeg (QA PASS; runner delta approval 1c27eff8).
- (e) Incremental SOURCE_PLAN test `GuidedIncrementalQualifiedPlanIT`, round 058 (patch only, not applied, no QA verdict).
- (f) Frontend regression test `pageIncrementalRecording.test.tsx`, round 065 (in tree; 3 tests pass; no QA verdict).
- Harness config: `studio.guided-authoring.translation.call-seconds` 140 to 45 for case436 and case437, a config-only ruling (round 062; local profile, no code change). The earlier 140 came from the case433 LOCAL24-precedent override.
- Catalog identity fix for code434 (round 059): the binder had copied the parent catalog pinning the old runner bundle hashes, so the rebuilt dist failed with `PROFILE_MISMATCH`; a code434-owned catalog (d7a63dce) was derived instead, loader proof new PASS / parent409 PROFILE_MISMATCH, G2 ACCEPT. The case was retargeted to case435.

### Debug Log — failed cases

- case430: not a failure. Prepared, accepted and started, then stopped by the user before RunGo (pause of 2026-10-22); consumed, no model call.
- case431: full learn ended `AUTHENTIC_CLARIFICATION_MISSING` (BUDGET_EXCEEDED, 24 proposals at step 2). Root cause: a labelled password input made the runner collector refuse every textbox/label hint, so the step-2 Username fill got `PREPARE_REFUSED TARGET_CHANGED` and the coordinator, which ignores non-2xx, re-asked the model until the budget ran out. Fixed by (a).
- case432: full learn ended `AUTHENTIC_CLARIFICATION_MISSING` (INVALID_CONTRACT, provider-side terminal). Root cause (inferred by two real-Vertex probes, not logged by the gateway client): the step-2 call hit the 60 s call deadline (probe 1: TIMEOUT 59977 ms); with 150 s the same call took 26 s. The case433 override of 140 s followed.
- case433: full learn, clarification and publication 1 passed; the incremental relearn failed `GUIDED_PUBLICATION_FAILED` (BUDGET_EXCEEDED at step 4). Root cause: the edited step 5 "Click Open in Primary workspace" yields a multi-token `targetIntent` that cannot satisfy exact-match target acquisition (confirmed by the G0 read: full run `targetIntent == "Open"`, incremental run 4+ tokens). Led to the user decision and fix (c). Its human pack covers 07-full only (PARTIAL).
- case434: consumed offline. The R11 pipeline catalog step failed `PROCESS_FAILED_NO_RETRY` with `PROFILE_MISMATCH` (stale parent catalog bundle hashes, see the catalog fix above); nothing was live. Missed by the devops prep and by QA G1 and G2.
- case435: full learn ended `AUTHENTIC_QUESTION_TIMEOUT`. Root cause: three provider calls stalled for the whole 140 s call budget (lease 139 s expired first, attempts SUPERSEDED with null tokens) and used 420 s of the 600 s session TTL; the immediate retry took 2-10 s. Mechanism about 95% (`G/round-062/dev-rca/RCA.md`); why the provider stalls is unknown. The harness config went to 45 s.
- case436: the product passed full and incremental (incl. the qualified step 5, publication epoch 2 rev 3) but the harness failed on the incremental seek, `PRIVATE_ASSERTION_OR_RUNTIME_FAILURE`, replay not run. Root cause: the graceful browser close took more than 3 s, so the hard teardown SIGKILLed ffmpeg and left an unfinalized WebM (no Duration/Cues), Chrome reported duration Infinity and the harness duration check failed (mechanism high, sole cause medium-high). The "Learning from step 5" assertion suspect, the harness video GET timeout and the frontend page path were refuted (`G/round-064/`, `G/round-065/dev-frontend/RCA.md`). Fixed by (d), with (f) as the frontend guard.

### Deferred, non-blocking findings (none closed, none waived)

- UX defects from `G/round-066/ux-png-review/NOTES.md` (functional scope only, ADR-SC-006): D1 generic "Click control" labels and UUID-like values in the learned-steps list (07-12); D2 no persistent seek marker; D3 step 5 unflagged and "Completed" shown beside the relearn note (09); D4 amber "Saved" chip (04); D5 toast covers Collapse (04, 09); D6 framing of 08/12; D7 stale "No run history" chip even after a PASSED replay (`ChecksStudioPage.tsx:1154`, `lastRunStatus`; cause is an inference); D8 "No run is on screen yet" during a live run; the 03 page shift under the side menu (new, cosmetic). D9 (the "Learning from step 5" suspect) is resolved as a flow blocker, but the line is still in no capture.
- Provider send-stall, Candidate B (`G/round-062/dev-rca/candidate-B-send-cap/`): a `send-timeout-seconds` cap (30) classified as `TRANSPORT_STALL`, definite-transient, into the existing retry. It extends the round-053 ruling b and **needs an @architect ruling**; Candidate A (record the overrun as REJECTED/TIMEOUT) is not recommended alone. No rebuild was done.
- Why the graceful browser close takes more than 3 s: unproven (RCA confidence low).
- @qa recommendations R1-R6 (`G/round-057/qa-qualified/QA-GATE.md`): R1 covered by RS71-E (patch not applied); R2 promote the cross-language lowering probe to `ObservedArtifactLowererTest` (open); R3 shared vectors for a non-landmark `regionId` and a non-ASCII case pair (open, defensive); R4 see the next item; R5 a stated region that matches nothing is a 30 s wait ending in BUDGET_EXCEEDED, better message deferred; R6 run the remaining `GuidedPublicationReplayIT` cases and `ObservedCoRunIT` before the final push (open).
- `./gradlew spotlessCheck checkstyleMain checkstyleTest` is red in the combined tree because of untracked `GuidedRefusalDiagnostics*`, `GuidedNextActionGuardDiagnosticsTest`, `GuidedRefusalCompatibilityTest`, `GuidedRefusalDiagnosticsTest`, `GuidedResponseParser:31` and four long lines in `GuidedProposalValidator` (QA R4). Their owner must format them before anyone claims `check` green or pushes. Not re-run by this bookkeeping.

- Other recorded follow-ups: the coordinator ignores non-2xx and there is no per-step no-progress ceiling (@architect/@po); unresolvable password labels (nested div, CSS asterisk) still poison hints fail-closed; the latent step-6 qualified `targetIntent` risk (@analyst); the multi-level qualifier, the clearer no-match message and the early-fail variants; the `<var>` contract paragraph of ruling c.

### Process deviations (stated honestly)

- Round 058: the construct step listed the key names and sizes (no values) of `compiled-runtime-pins.private.json`, a private file.
